## ![](_page_0_Picture_6.jpeg)

## From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia

## ![](_page_0_Picture_8.jpeg)

[Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Everything_Everywhere_All_at_Once) (2022) is A24's highestgrossing film and marks their third collaboration with the directing duo [Daniels](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daniels_(directors)) (pictured).

[A24](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/A24) is a film distribution and production company based in New York City that launched in August 2012. It released its first film, [A Glimpse Inside the Mind of Charles Swan III](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/A_Glimpse_Inside_the_Mind_of_Charles_Swan_III), in February 2013, and gained recognition with the box-office success of [Spring Breakers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spring_Breakers) that March[.\[1\]](#page-1-0)[\[2\]](#page-1-1) They entered into deals with [Amazon Prime Video](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amazon_Prime_Video) and [DirecTV Cinema](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/DirecTV_Cinema) in late 2013 for the video-on-demand release of some films, and, in 2016, amassed seven [Academy Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award) nominations for movies they distributed; [Amy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Amy_(2015_film)) won [Best Documentary,](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award_for_Best_Documentary_Feature_Film) [Ex Machina](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ex_Machina_(film)) won [Best](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award_for_Best_Visual_Effects) [Visual Effects,](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award_for_Best_Visual_Effects) and [Brie Larson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brie_Larson) received [Best Actress](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award_for_Best_Actress) for [Room](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Room_(2015_film)).

[\[2\]](#page-1-1)[\[3\]](#page-1-2) A24 also became a [production studio](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Production_studio) that year, financing [Moonlight](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moonlight_(2016_film)) in partnership with [Plan B Entertainment;](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Plan_B_Entertainment) [\[4\]](#page-2-0) that film earned critical acclaim and won three Academy Awards, including [Best Picture.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Academy_Award_for_Best_Picture) [\[5\]](#page-2-1) Since then, the company began producing more original content and announced partnerships with [Apple TV+](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Apple_TV%2B) and [Showtime Networks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Showtime_Networks) for the digital releases of some of its films[.\[6\]](#page-2-2)[\[7\]](#page-2-3) [Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Everything_Everywhere_All_at_Once) (2022) is its highest-grossing film with \$143 million in box office earnings and was the recipient of [numerous accolades](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_accolades_received_by_Everything_Everywhere_All_at_Once) and seven Academy Awards, including Best Picture.[\[8\]](#page-2-4)[\[9\]](#page-2-5) As of 2022, [Lady Bird](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lady_Bird_(film)) (2017) and [Eighth Grade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eighth_Grade_(film)) (2018) are A24's highest-rated films on [Rotten Tomatoes,](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rotten_Tomatoes) with a 99% approval rating for each, and Moonlight is one of the highest-scoring films on [Metacritic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Metacritic) with a 99 out of 100[.\[10\]](#page-2-6)[\[11\]](#page-2-7)
